Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The data_structures variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 247 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 427 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the data_structures space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 247 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 427. QED.
